    trigger.classList.toggle('is-active', !!state.quickPanel);
    trigger.classList.toggle('is-manager-open', !!state.modal);
    trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(!!state.quickPanel));
    trigger.setAttribute('aria-label',`WISH · ${monitor.armed?'주입 켜짐':'주입 꺼짐'}${monitor.busy?' · 반영 중':''} · 짧게 누르면 빠른 관리, 길게 누르면 전체 RP Manager`);
    trigger.title=`WISH · ${monitor.armed?'주입 켜짐':'주입 꺼짐'}${monitor.warning?' · 확인할 일 있음':''} · 짧게 누르면 빠른 관리, 길게 누르면 전체 RP Manager`;
    trigger.querySelector('.rpcm-header-monitor-label').textContent='WISH';
    if (state.modal) trigger.rpcmCancelHold?.();
    trigger.disabled = !!state.modal;
  }

  function newMessagesSinceLastScan(room, recentMessages) {
    const recent = (recentMessages || []).filter(m => ['user','assistant'].includes(messageRoleOf(m)));
    if (!recent.length) return [];
    const lastId = String(room.autoScanLastMessageId || '');
    let fresh;
    if (!lastId) fresh = recent.slice(0, Math.min(APP.autoScanMessageLimit, recent.length));
    else {
      const idx = recent.findIndex(m => String(messageIdOf(m) || '') === lastId);
      fresh = idx >= 0 ? recent.slice(0, idx) : recent.slice(0, Math.min(4, recent.length));
    }
    room.autoScanLastMessageId = String(messageIdOf(recent[0]) || room.autoScanLastMessageId || '');
    return fresh;
  }

  async function autoDetectCharacters(room, freshMessages) {
    if (!room.autoCharacterDetection) return { detected: [], added: 0, reset: 0 };
    let library;
    try{library=room.autoCharacterLibraryId?await getCharacterLibrary(room.autoCharacterLibraryId):null;}
    catch(error){console.warn('[Wish] 캐릭터 자동 감지 대기: 설정집 조회 실패',error);return {detected:[],added:0,reset:0,paused:true};}
    // An unavailable library is not evidence that every character left the scene.
    if(!Array.isArray(library?.characters)||!library.characters.length)return {detected:[],added:0,reset:0,paused:true};
    // Manager/로어가 숨겨 주입한 기억은 후보 근거로 쓰지 않습니다.
    // 마커 블록을 제거하고 남은 실제 RP 본문에서만 등록 이름·별칭을 찾습니다.
    const text = (freshMessages || []).map(m => stripAutomationNoise(messageTextOf(m), true)).filter(Boolean).join('\n\n');
    const chars = (room.slots || []).filter(s => s.group === 'character');
    const byKey = new Map(chars.map(s => [libraryItemKey(s), s]));
    const detected = [];
    let added = 0, reset = 0;

    for (const src of (library?.characters || [])) {
      const key = libraryItemKey(src);
      let slot = byKey.get(key);
      const evidence = text ? characterRpDetectionEvidence(text, src) : { accepted:false };
      const matched = evidence.accepted ? evidence.matched : null;
      // 📌 자동 고정은 '감지 이벤트'가 아니라 항상 유지되는 사용자 상태입니다.
      // 매 폴링마다 감지된 것으로 반환하면 토스트가 반복되고 모달이 재렌더링되어 스크롤이 위로 튀므로
      // 실제 최근 RP에서 이름/별칭이 잡힌 경우에만 detected에 포함합니다.
      if (!matched) continue;
      if (slot?.autoExcluded) continue;
      if (!slot) {
        slot = makeDynamicSlot('character', String(src.title || '캐릭터'));
        slot.aliases = Array.isArray(src.aliases) ? [...src.aliases] : [];
        slot.content = String(src.content || '');
        slot.retentionTurns = normalizeRetentionTurns(src.retentionTurns);
        slot.autoExcluded = false;
        slot.autoPinned = false;
        room.slots.push(slot);
        byKey.set(key, slot);
        added++;
      }
      if (!String(slot.content || '').trim()) slot.content = String(src.content || '');
      if (!Array.isArray(slot.aliases) || !slot.aliases.length) slot.aliases = Array.isArray(src.aliases) ? [...src.aliases] : [];
      if (slot.manualInjectionOverride === 'off') continue;
      slot.enabled = true;
      slot.autoChosenByRp = true;
      slot.lastAutoMatch = matched;
      slot.lastAutoConfidence = Number(evidence.confidence || 0);
      slot.lastAutoReason = String(evidence.reason || '실제 RP 본문 감지');
      slot.lastAutoDetectedAt = Date.now();
      detected.push({ slot, matched, confidence:slot.lastAutoConfidence, reason:slot.lastAutoReason });

      if (room.pending) {
        const items = Array.isArray(room.pending.items) ? room.pending.items : (room.pending.items = []);
        const idx = items.findIndex(i => i.slotId === slot.id);
        const next = slotToPendingItem(slot);
        next.autoType = 'character';
        next.matchedAlias = matched;
        next.autoConfidence = slot.lastAutoConfidence;
        next.autoDetectionReason = slot.lastAutoReason;
        next.recallReason = `신뢰도 ${slot.lastAutoConfidence}% · 감지: ${slot.lastAutoReason}`;
        if (idx < 0) items.push(next);
        else if (room.autoCharacterResetOnReappear || matched === '사용자 고정') { items[idx] = next; reset++; }
      }
    }

    // Clear previous auto-selection when the character has left the recent scene.
    const currentIds = new Set(detected.map(entry => entry.slot.id));
    for (const slot of (room.slots || []).filter(s => s.group === 'character')) {
      if (slot.autoPinned || slot.manualInjectionOverride === 'on' || !rpAutomaticSlot(room,slot)) continue;
      if (!currentIds.has(slot.id)) {
        slot.enabled = false;
        if (room.pending) room.pending.items = (room.pending.items || []).filter(item => item.slotId !== slot.id);
      }
    }

    // 설정집에 없는 현재 방 캐릭터라도 사용자가 📌 고정했다면 조용히 항상 유지합니다.
    // 고정 자체는 자동 '감지'로 취급하지 않아 반복 토스트/모달 재렌더링을 발생시키지 않습니다.
    for (const slot of (room.slots || []).filter(s => s.group === 'character' && s.autoPinned)) {
      if (detected.some(x => x.slot.id === slot.id)) continue;
      slot.enabled = true;
      slot.lastAutoMatch = '사용자 고정';
      if (room.pending) {
        const items = Array.isArray(room.pending.items) ? room.pending.items : (room.pending.items = []);
        const idx = items.findIndex(i => i.slotId === slot.id);
        const next = slotToPendingItem(slot);
        next.autoType = 'character';
        next.matchedAlias = '사용자 고정';
        next.recallReason = '사용자 고정';
        if (idx < 0) items.push(next); else items[idx] = next;
      }
    }
    normalizeRoomSlots(room);
    return { detected, added, reset };
  }

  function autoDetectImportantItems(room, freshMessages) {
    if (!room.autoItemDetection) return { detected: [], reset: 0 };
    // 숨김 주입문·상태창·외부 로어 참고 블록은 제외하고 실제 RP 문장만 검사합니다.
    const text = (freshMessages || []).map(m => stripAutomationNoise(messageTextOf(m), true)).filter(Boolean).join('\n\n');
    const detected = [];
    let reset = 0;
    for (const slot of (room.slots || []).filter(s => s.group === 'item')) {
      if (slot.autoExcluded || !String(slot.content || '').trim()) continue;
      const evidence = text ? importantItemRpDetectionEvidence(text, slot) : { accepted:false };
      if (!evidence.accepted) continue;
      if (slot.manualInjectionOverride === 'off') continue;
      slot.enabled = true;
      slot.autoChosenByRp = true;
      slot.lastAutoMatch = evidence.matched;
      slot.lastAutoConfidence = Number(evidence.confidence || 0);
      slot.lastAutoReason = String(evidence.reason || '실제 RP 본문 물품 언급 감지');
      slot.lastAutoDetectedAt = Date.now();
      detected.push({ slot, matched:evidence.matched, confidence:slot.lastAutoConfidence, reason:slot.lastAutoReason });

      if (room.pending) {
        const items = Array.isArray(room.pending.items) ? room.pending.items : (room.pending.items = []);
        const idx = items.findIndex(item => item.slotId === slot.id);
        const next = slotToPendingItem(slot);
        next.autoType = 'important-item';
        next.matchedAlias = evidence.matched;
        next.autoConfidence = slot.lastAutoConfidence;
        next.autoDetectionReason = slot.lastAutoReason;
        next.recallReason = `신뢰도 ${slot.lastAutoConfidence}% · 감지: ${slot.lastAutoReason}`;
        if (idx < 0) items.push(next);
        else if (room.autoItemResetOnReappear) { items[idx] = next; reset++; }
      }
    }

    const currentIds = new Set(detected.map(entry => entry.slot.id));
    for (const slot of (room.slots || []).filter(s => s.group === 'item')) {
      if (slot.autoPinned || slot.manualInjectionOverride === 'on' || !rpAutomaticSlot(room,slot)) continue;
      if (!currentIds.has(slot.id)) {
        slot.enabled = false;
        if (room.pending) room.pending.items = (room.pending.items || []).filter(item => item.slotId !== slot.id);
      }
    }

    for (const slot of (room.slots || []).filter(s => s.group === 'item' && s.autoPinned)) {
      if (detected.some(entry => entry.slot.id === slot.id)) continue;
      slot.enabled = true;
      slot.lastAutoMatch = '사용자 고정';
      if (room.pending) {
        const items = Array.isArray(room.pending.items) ? room.pending.items : (room.pending.items = []);
        const idx = items.findIndex(item => item.slotId === slot.id);
        const next = slotToPendingItem(slot);
        next.autoType = 'important-item';
        next.matchedAlias = '사용자 고정';
        next.recallReason = '사용자 고정';
        if (idx < 0) items.push(next); else items[idx] = next;
      }
    }
    return { detected, reset };
  }

  function refreshAutoRecentLogsToPending(room) {
    if (!room.pending || !room.autoLogRecallEnabled) return 0;
    const slot = (room.slots || []).find(s => s.id === 'logSummary');
    if (!slot?.enabled || !String(slot.content || '').trim()) return 0;
    const blocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(slot.content);
    if (!blocks.length) return 0;

    const excludedKeys = new Set((room.autoLogExcludedKeys || []).map(String));
    const pinnedKeys = new Set((room.autoLogPinnedKeys || []).map(String));
    const manualKeys = new Set((room.manualLogSelectedKeys || []).map(String));
    const occupied = new Set([...pinnedKeys, ...manualKeys]);
    const eligible = autoRecallVisibleLogBlocks(blocks, room).filter(b => !excludedKeys.has(b.key));
    const latest = recentContinuityLogBlocks(room, eligible, excludedKeys);
    const scored = eligibleRelatedLogScores(room, eligible, room.autoRecallContextText || '', new Set([...excludedKeys, ...occupied]));
    const recent = latest.filter(block => !occupied.has(String(block.key)));

    // 활성 시간선의 최신 eligible 날짜 두 개를 직전 흐름으로 유지합니다.
    // 매 응답 뒤 최신 목록을 다시 계산해 오래된 자동 최근로그는 빼고, 새 최신 로그는 유지턴이
    // 끝났더라도 0턴부터 다시 시작시킵니다. 같은 항목이 아직 활성 상태라면 진행도는 이어갑니다.
    const items = Array.isArray(room.pending.items) ? room.pending.items : [];
    const previousRecent = items.filter(item => item?.autoType === 'recent-log');
    const previousBySource = new Map(previousRecent.map(item => [
      String(item.sourceKey || item.slotId || '').replace(/^auto-log:/, ''),
      item,
    ]));
    room.pending.items = items.filter(item => item?.autoType !== 'recent-log');

    for (const block of recent) {
      const rank = scored.findIndex(entry => entry.block.key === block.key);
      const next = makeRecentContinuityLogItem(block, slot, rank < 0 ? null : scored[rank], rank < 0 ? null : rank + 1, scored.length);
      const previous = previousBySource.get(String(block.key));
      const previousTotal = Number(previous?.totalTurns || 0);
      const previousUsed = Number(previous?.usedTurns || 0);
      const previousActive = !!previous && (previousTotal === 0 || previousUsed < previousTotal);
      if (previousActive) next.usedTurns = previousUsed;
      room.pending.items.push(next);
    }
    return recent.length;
  }

  const AI_CONTEXT_RESPONSE_SCHEMA = Object.freeze({
    type:'OBJECT',
    properties:{
      situation:{ type:'STRING' },
      keys:{ type:'ARRAY', items:{ type:'STRING' } },
      decisions:{type:'ARRAY',items:{type:'OBJECT',properties:{key:{type:'STRING'},reason:{type:'STRING'}},required:['key','reason']}},
    },
    required:['situation','keys'],
  });

  function safeAiContextRawExcerpt(value) {
    return normalizeLineBreaks(String(value || '')).trim().slice(0, 1200)
      .replace(/AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{20,}/g, '[인증정보 숨김]')
      .replace(/((?:api[_-]?key|private[_-]?key|client[_-]?email|project[_-]?id)\s*["']?\s*[:=]\s*)[^,\n}]+/gi, '$1[인증정보 숨김]');
  }

  function parseAiContextResponse(value) {
    const raw = normalizeLineBreaks(String(value || '')).trim();
    if (!raw) throw new Error('응답 형식 오류: 빈 응답입니다.');
    let jsonText = raw;
    const fenced = raw.match(/^```(?:json)?[ \t]*\n([\s\S]*?)\n```[ \t]*$/i);
    if (fenced) jsonText = fenced[1].trim();
    else if (/^```/.test(raw)) throw new Error('응답 형식 오류: JSON 코드블록 형식을 확인할 수 없습니다.');
    let parsed;
    try { parsed = JSON.parse(jsonText); }
    catch (_) { throw new Error('응답 형식 오류: 전체 응답이 하나의 JSON 객체가 아닙니다.'); }
    if (!parsed || Array.isArray(parsed) || typeof parsed !== 'object' || typeof parsed.situation !== 'string' || !Array.isArray(parsed.keys)) {
      throw new Error('응답 형식 오류: {"situation":"...","keys":[...]} schema가 필요합니다.');
    }
    if (Object.keys(parsed).some(key => !['situation','keys','decisions'].includes(key)) || parsed.keys.some(key => typeof key !== 'string')) {
      throw new Error('응답 형식 오류: situation·keys·decisions 이외의 값이 포함되었습니다.');
    }
    if(parsed.decisions!==undefined&&(!Array.isArray(parsed.decisions)||parsed.decisions.some(row=>!row||typeof row.key!=='string'||typeof row.reason!=='string')))throw new Error('응답 형식 오류: 후보별 이유 형식을 확인해 주세요.');
    const situation = normalizeLineBreaks(parsed.situation).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    if (!situation) throw new Error('응답 형식 오류: 현재 상황 설명이 비어 있습니다.');
    return {
      situation:situation.slice(0, 500),
      keys:parsed.keys.map(key => key.trim()).filter(Boolean),
      decisions:(parsed.decisions||[]).map(row=>({key:row.key.trim(),reason:row.reason.trim().slice(0,240)})),
    };
  }

  function assertAiContextResponseComplete(result) {
    const finishReason = String(result?.finishReason || '').trim();
    if (/(?:MAX_TOKENS|LENGTH|TOKEN_LIMIT|INCOMPLETE)/i.test(finishReason)) {
      throw new Error(`응답 출력 한도 초과: JSON이 중간에 끊겼습니다. finishReason=${finishReason}`);
    }
  }

  async function aiRerankRelatedLogCandidates(room, contextText, candidates, count, options={}) {
    if (!room?.aiContextLogRerankEnabled || (!candidates.length&&!options.manual)) return candidates;
    const settings = aiFeatureSettings(loadAiSummarySettings(), 'context');
    const provider = settings.provider;
    const secret = readAiSecret(provider);
    const candidateText = candidates.slice(0, 12).map((entry, index) => {
      const block = entry.block;
      return `[후보 ${index + 1}]\nKEY=${block.key}\n시간선=${logTimelineLabelOfBlock(block)}\n제목=${block.heading}\n내용=${String(block.body || '').slice(0, 2200)}`;
    }).join('\n\n');
    const prompt = `먼저 [현재 RP]만 읽고, 지금 진행 중인 상황을 확인된 사실만으로 한국어 1~2문장(최대 220자)으로 정리한다. 누가 누구에게 무엇을 하고 있으며 어떤 사건·대화·쟁점이 진행 중인지 구체적으로 쓴다. 감정·의도·행동을 추측하거나 아직 발생하지 않은 일을 확정하지 않는다. [${semanticEnabled(room)?"하이브리드 검색 후보":"키워드 검색 후보"}]의 과거 내용을 현재 상황에 섞지 않는다.\n\n그다음 현재 RP 맥락과 실제로 이어지는 날짜로그를 고른다. 인물 이름만 겹치는 로그보다 사건·장소·물건·관계 변화·미해결 단서가 이어지는 로그를 우선한다. 후보에 없는 KEY는 만들지 않는다. 최대 ${count}개를 관련도순으로 고른다. 적합한 로그가 없으면 keys는 빈 배열로 반환한다.\n\n[현재 RP]\n${String(contextText || '').slice(-12000)}\n\n[${semanticEnabled(room)?"하이브리드 검색 후보":"키워드 검색 후보"}]\n${candidateText}\n\n선택 및 미선택 후보 각각에 대해 decisions에 KEY와 구체적인 이유를 한국어 한 문장(최대 80자)으로 적는다. 현재 사건과의 연결 또는 연결 부족을 입력에서만 설명한다. 후보가 없으면 현재 상황만 정리하고 keys와 decisions는 빈 배열로 반환한다.\nJSON 한 줄만 출력:\n{"situation":"현재 RP에서 확인된 상황","keys":["후보의 KEY"],"decisions":[{"key":"후보의 KEY","reason":"선택 또는 미선택 이유"}]}`;
    const systemInstruction = '현재 RP 상황을 사실대로 정리하고 날짜로그 후보를 맥락 기준으로 재정렬하는 선택기다. 설명 없이 지정된 JSON만 출력한다.';
    const callOptions = { feature:'context', room, responseJsonSchema:AI_CONTEXT_RESPONSE_SCHEMA, allowEmptyText:true, thinkingLevel:'low' };
    let result = await callAiSummaryProvider(provider, settings, secret, systemInstruction, prompt, 4096, () => {}, callOptions);
    let parsedResponse;
    let formatRetried = false;
    try {
      assertAiContextResponseComplete(result);
      parsedResponse = parseAiContextResponse(result.text);
    } catch (firstError) {
      formatRetried = true;
      const firstRaw = safeAiContextRawExcerpt(result.text);
      const retryPrompt = `${prompt}\n\n[응답 형식 재요청]\nJSON 외 텍스트 출력 금지. 설명 금지. 코드블록 금지. {"situation":"현재 RP에서 확인된 상황","keys":["후보의 KEY"]} schema만 반환한다.`;
      let retryResult = null;
      try {
        retryResult = await callAiSummaryProvider(provider, settings, secret, systemInstruction, retryPrompt, 4096, () => {}, callOptions);
        assertAiContextResponseComplete(retryResult);
        parsedResponse = parseAiContextResponse(retryResult.text);
        result = retryResult;
      } catch (retryError) {
        const failure = new Error(`응답 형식 오류: 1차 파싱 실패 후 자동 재요청도 실패했습니다. ${String(retryError?.message || retryError || '')}`.trim());
        failure.aiRaw = [firstRaw, safeAiContextRawExcerpt(retryResult?.text || '')].filter(Boolean).join('\n\n[자동 재요청 응답]\n');
        failure.parseFailure = `${String(firstError?.message || firstError)} / ${String(retryError?.message || retryError)}`;
        failure.finishReason = String(retryResult?.finishReason || result?.finishReason || '');
        throw failure;
      }
    }
    const byKey = new Map(candidates.slice(0, 12).map(entry => [String(entry.block.key), entry]));
    const selected = [];
    for (const key of parsedResponse.keys) {
      const entry = byKey.get(key);
      if (entry && !selected.includes(entry)) selected.push(entry);
      if (selected.length >= count) break;
    }
    const totalLogs = parseDatedLogBlocks((room.slots || []).find(slot => slot.id === 'logSummary')?.content || '').length;
    room.aiContextReviewReport = {
      state:'success', checkedAt:nowIso(), totalLogs, candidateCount:candidates.length, inspectedCount:Math.min(12, candidates.length), selectedCount:selected.length,
      provider, model:String(result.model || settings.models?.[provider] || ''), fallback:false, formatRetried,
      managerVersion:APP.version, finishReason:String(result.finishReason || ''),
      situation:parsedResponse.situation,
      keywords:[...new Set(selected.flatMap(item => [...(item.matchedPhrases || []), ...(item.matchedCoreTokens || []), ...(item.matchedRareTokens || [])]))].slice(0, 12),
      characters:[...new Set(selected.flatMap(item => item.matchedCharacterTerms || []))].slice(0, 8),
      execution:options.manual?'수동 실행':'자동 실행',note:!candidates.length?'과거 후보가 없어 현재 RP 상황만 AI로 확인했습니다. 최근 연속성 로그는 별도로 유지합니다.':'',
      candidates:candidates.slice(0,12).map((item,index)=>({key:String(item.block.key),title:String(item.block.titleText||item.block.heading||''),selected:selected.includes(item),candidateRank:index+1,keywordScore:Number(item.keywordScore||0),semanticScore:Number(item.semanticScore||0),reason:parsedResponse.decisions.find(row=>row.key===String(item.block.key))?.reason||(selected.includes(item)?'AI가 현재 맥락과 연결되는 후보로 선택':'AI가 선택하지 않음 · 구체적 이유 미제공')})),
      items:selected.map((item, index) => ({ key:String(item.block.key), title:String(item.block.titleText || item.block.heading || ''), rank:index + 1, candidateRank:candidates.indexOf(item) + 1, score:Number(item.score || 0), reason:relatedLogReason(item) })),
    };
    setAiFeatureStatus('context', 'ok', provider, room.aiContextReviewReport.model, '최근 검토 정상');
    return selected;
  }

  function recordAiContextFallback(room, error, candidates, selected) {
    const failure = classifyAiFailure(error);
    const settings = aiFeatureSettings(loadAiSummarySettings(), 'context');
    const provider = settings.provider;
    const totalLogs = parseDatedLogBlocks((room.slots || []).find(slot => slot.id === 'logSummary')?.content || '').length;
    room.aiContextReviewReport = {
      state:'fallback', checkedAt:nowIso(), totalLogs, candidateCount:(candidates || []).length, inspectedCount:Math.min(12, (candidates || []).length), selectedCount:(selected || []).length,
      provider, model:String(settings.models?.[provider] || ''), fallback:true, failureCategory:failure.category, failureDetail:failure.detail,
      managerVersion:APP.version, finishReason:String(error?.finishReason || ''),
      situation:'',
      failureRaw:safeAiContextRawExcerpt(error?.aiRaw || ''), parseFailure:String(error?.parseFailure || ''),
      items:(selected || []).map((item, index) => ({ key:String(item.block.key), title:String(item.block.titleText || item.block.heading || ''), rank:index + 1, candidateRank:(candidates || []).indexOf(item) + 1, score:Number(item.score || 0), reason:relatedLogReason(item) })),
    };
    setAiFeatureStatus('context', 'error', provider, room.aiContextReviewReport.model, failure.category, failure.detail);
    return failure;
  }

  function aiContextSelectionReason(room,key,fallback){
    const report=room.aiContextReviewReport;
    return report?.state==='success'?report.candidates?.find(row=>row.key===String(key)&&row.selected)?.reason||fallback:fallback;
  }

  function recordAiContextSkipped(room,reason){
    const settings=aiFeatureSettings(loadAiSummarySettings(),'context');
    room.aiContextReviewReport={state:'skipped',checkedAt:nowIso(),managerVersion:APP.version,totalLogs:parseDatedLogBlocks((room.slots||[]).find(s=>s.id==='logSummary')?.content||'').length,candidateCount:0,inspectedCount:0,selectedCount:0,provider:settings.provider,model:settings.models?.[settings.provider]||'',note:reason,situation:'',items:[],candidates:[],apiCalled:false};
    return room.aiContextReviewReport;
  }
  function manualAiContextCandidates(room,blocks,context,skip){
    const scored=scoreRelatedLogBlocks(blocks,context,skip,room),known=new Set(scored.map(row=>String(row.block.key))),active=normalizedLogTimelineKey(activeLogTimelineLabel(room));
    // Explicit manual execution may ask AI to assess a small broader pool; no automatic injection is implied.
    const extra=blocks.filter(b=>!skip.has(String(b.key))&&!known.has(String(b.key))&&normalizedLogTimelineKey(logTimelineLabelOfBlock(b))===active).sort((a,b)=>b.index-a.index).slice(0,12).map(block=>({block,score:0,coreScore:0,keywordScore:0,semanticScore:0,matchedCoreTokens:[],matchedCharacterTerms:[]}));
    return [...scored,...extra].slice(0,12);
  }

  async function refreshAiContextReviewNow(room) {
    if (!room?.aiContextLogRerankEnabled) throw new Error('AI 맥락 검토가 꺼져 있습니다. API 설정에서 먼저 켜 주세요.');
    if (!room.autoLogRecallEnabled) throw new Error('최근·관련 로그 자동 선택이 꺼져 있습니다. 먼저 켜 주세요.');
    const slot = (room.slots || []).find(item => item.id === 'logSummary');
    if (!slot?.enabled || !String(slot.content || '').trim()) throw new Error('검토할 날짜로그가 없습니다.');

    const recent = await fetchRecentMessages(apiChatIdOf(room), Math.max(12, APP.autoScanMessageLimit));
    const contextText = recent
      .filter(message => ['user','assistant'].includes(messageRoleOf(message)))
      .map(message => stripAutomationNoise(messageTextOf(message)))
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join('\n')
      .slice(-12000);
    if (!contextText) throw new Error('현재 RP 맥락을 불러오지 못했습니다.');
    room.autoRecallContextText = contextText;
    prepareLocalRecall(room,contextText,recent);
    await prepareSemanticRecall(room,contextText,recent);
    room.aiContextReviewReport = null;
    const before=injectionRecheckSnapshot(room);

    if (room.pending) {
      await addAutoRelatedLogsToPending(room, contextText,{manual:true});
      await syncPendingCarrier(room, 'manual-ai-context-refresh');
    } else {
      const previewBudget = contextBudgetForPreview(room);
      const baseItems = snapshotSelectedItems(room, contextText, previewBudget);
      const previewItems=await rerankInitialRelatedLogItems(room,baseItems,contextText,previewBudget,{manual:true});
      recordRecallSelectionAudit(room,before,previewItems,contextText,'수동 맥락 검토 · 다음 주입 후보',false);
      await saveRoom(room);
    }
    return room.aiContextReviewReport;
  }

  async function rerankInitialRelatedLogItems(room, items, contextText, contextBudget, options={}) {
    if (!room?.aiContextLogRerankEnabled || !room.autoLogRecallEnabled) return items;
    const slot = (room.slots || []).find(s => s.id === 'logSummary');
    if (!slot?.enabled || !String(slot.content || '').trim()) return items;
    const blocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(slot.content);
    if (!blocks.length) return items;

    const excludedKeys = new Set((room.autoLogExcludedKeys || []).map(String));
    const pinnedKeys = new Set((room.autoLogPinnedKeys || []).map(String));
    const manualKeys = new Set((room.manualLogSelectedKeys || []).map(String));
    const eligible = autoRecallVisibleLogBlocks(blocks, room).filter(block => !excludedKeys.has(block.key));
    const occupied = new Set([...pinnedKeys, ...manualKeys]);
    const existingNonRelatedKeys = new Set((items || []).filter(item => item.autoType !== 'related-log').map(item => String(item.sourceKey || '')));
    const skip = new Set([...excludedKeys, ...occupied, ...existingNonRelatedKeys]);
    let relatedCandidates = eligibleRelatedLogScores(room, eligible, contextText, skip);
    if(!relatedLogInjectionCount(room)){recordAiContextSkipped(room,'관련로그 개수가 0으로 설정되어 AI 검토를 건너뛰었습니다. 최근 로그는 별도 유지합니다.');return items;}
    if(!relatedCandidates.length&&options.manual)relatedCandidates=manualAiContextCandidates(room,eligible,contextText,skip);
    if (!relatedCandidates.length&&!options.manual){recordAiContextSkipped(room,'검색 기준을 통과한 과거 후보가 없습니다. 최근 연속성·항상·직접 주입 로그는 별도 유지합니다. 수동 재검토는 더 넓은 후보를 확인합니다.');return items;}

    let selected;
    try {
      const reranked = await aiRerankRelatedLogCandidates(room, contextText, relatedCandidates, Math.min(12, relatedLogInjectionCount(room)),options);
      selected = reranked;
    } catch (error) {
      const fallback = relatedCandidates;
      const failure = recordAiContextFallback(room, error, relatedCandidates, fallback);
      console.warn('[RP Manager] 최초 주입 AI 맥락 검토 실패, 키워드 결과를 사용합니다.', error);
      notify(`AI 맥락 검토 실패 · ${failure.category}\n${semanticEnabled(room)?'하이브리드 검색':'기본 키워드 방식'}으로 대체됨`, 'warn', 6500);
      return items;
    }

    const nonLogs = (items || []).filter(item => item.sourceSlotId !== 'logSummary' && item.group !== 'log-auto' && item.slotId !== 'logSummary');
    const fixedLogs = (items || []).filter(item => (item.sourceSlotId === 'logSummary' || item.group === 'log-auto' || item.slotId === 'logSummary') && item.autoType !== 'related-log');
    const aiRelated = selected.slice(0, relatedLogInjectionCount(room)).map((scored, index) => makeLogRecallItem(
      scored.block,
      slot,
      'related-log',
      '관련로그',
      `${logTimelineLabelOfBlock(scored.block)} · AI 맥락 검토 · ${aiContextSelectionReason(room,scored.block.key,relatedLogReason(scored))}`,
      {
        score:scored.score,
        coreScore:scored.coreScore, keywordScore:scored.keywordScore, semanticScore:scored.semanticScore, ...localRecallExtras(scored),
        characterScore:scored.characterScore,
        rank:index + 1,
        candidateCount:relatedCandidates.length,
        matchedTerms:[...(scored.matchedPhrases || []), ...(scored.matchedCoreTokens || []), ...(scored.matchedCharacterTerms || [])],
        matchedCoreTerms:[...(scored.matchedPhrases || []), ...(scored.matchedCoreTokens || []), ...(scored.matchedRareTokens || [])],
        matchedCharacterTerms:[...(scored.matchedCharacterTerms || [])],
      },
    ));
    return [...nonLogs, ...fitLogItemsToBudget(room, nonLogs, [...fixedLogs, ...aiRelated], contextBudget)];
  }

  async function addAutoRelatedLogsToPending(room, contextText, options={}) {
    if(room){prepareLocalRecall(room,contextText);await prepareSemanticRecall(room,contextText);}
    if (!room.pending || !room.autoLogRecallEnabled) return 0;
    const slot = (room.slots || []).find(s => s.id === 'logSummary');
    if (!slot?.enabled || !String(slot.content || '').trim()) return 0;
    const blocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(slot.content);
    if (!blocks.length) return 0;
    const excludedKeys = new Set((room.autoLogExcludedKeys || []).map(String));
    const pinnedKeys = new Set((room.autoLogPinnedKeys || []).map(String));
    const manualKeys = new Set((room.manualLogSelectedKeys || []).map(String));
    const eligible = autoRecallVisibleLogBlocks(blocks, room).filter(b => !excludedKeys.has(b.key));
    const occupied = new Set([...pinnedKeys, ...manualKeys]);
    const latestKeys = new Set(recentContinuityLogBlocks(room, eligible, excludedKeys).map(block => String(block.key)));
    const allEligible = eligibleRelatedLogScores(room, eligible, contextText, new Set([...excludedKeys, ...occupied]));
    let relatedCandidates = allEligible.filter(entry => !latestKeys.has(String(entry.block.key)));
    if(!relatedCandidates.length&&options.manual)relatedCandidates=manualAiContextCandidates(room,eligible,contextText,new Set([...excludedKeys,...occupied,...latestKeys]));
    let related = relatedCandidates;
    if(room.aiContextLogRerankEnabled&&(!relatedLogInjectionCount(room)||(!relatedCandidates.length&&!options.manual)))recordAiContextSkipped(room,!relatedLogInjectionCount(room)?'관련로그 개수가 0입니다.':'검색 기준을 통과한 과거 후보가 없습니다. 최근 연속성 로그는 별도로 유지합니다.');
    if (room.aiContextLogRerankEnabled && relatedLogInjectionCount(room) > 0 && (relatedCandidates.length||options.manual)) {
      try {
        const reranked = await aiRerankRelatedLogCandidates(room, contextText, relatedCandidates, Math.min(12, relatedLogInjectionCount(room)),options);
        related = reranked;
      } catch (error) {
        const fallback = allEligible.filter(entry=>!latestKeys.has(String(entry.block.key)));
        const failure = recordAiContextFallback(room, error, relatedCandidates, fallback);
        related = fallback;
        console.warn('[RP Manager] AI 맥락 검토 실패, 키워드 결과를 사용합니다.', error);
        notify(`AI 맥락 검토 실패 · ${failure.category}\n${semanticEnabled(room)?'하이브리드 검색':'기본 키워드 방식'}으로 대체됨`, 'warn', 6500);
      }
    }

    // 이전 턴의 관련로그를 계속 쌓지 않고 이번 검색 결과로 교체합니다.
    // 같은 로그가 다시 선정되면 남은 유지턴은 이어가되, 순위에서 빠진 로그는 즉시 제외합니다.
    const items = Array.isArray(room.pending.items) ? room.pending.items : [];
    const previousRelated = items.filter(item => item?.autoType === 'related-log');
    const previousBySource = new Map(previousRelated.map(item => [
      String(item.sourceKey || item.slotId || '').replace(/^auto-log:/, ''),
      item,
    ]));
    room.pending.items = items.filter(item => item?.autoType !== 'related-log');
    const nextItems = room.pending.items;
    let added = 0;
    for (let relatedIndex = 0; relatedIndex < Math.min(relatedLogInjectionCount(room), related.length); relatedIndex++) {
      const scored = related[relatedIndex];
      const b = scored.block;
      // 최초 주입 경로와 자동 갱신 경로가 같은 메타데이터 구조를 사용하게 해서
      // 총점만 있고 핵심/인물 점수·매칭어·후보순위가 사라지는 현상을 막습니다.
      const selectionMethod = room.aiContextLogRerankEnabled
        ? (room.aiContextReviewReport?.state === 'success' ? 'AI 맥락 검토로 선정' : (semanticEnabled(room)?'AI 검토 실패 후 하이브리드 선정':'AI 검토 실패 후 키워드 선정'))
        : (semanticEnabled(room)?'AI 맥락 검토 OFF · 하이브리드 검색':'AI 맥락 검토 OFF · 기본 검색');
      const next = makeLogRecallItem(b, slot, 'related-log', '관련로그', `${logTimelineLabelOfBlock(b)} · ${selectionMethod} · ${aiContextSelectionReason(room,b.key,relatedLogReason(scored))}`, {
        score: scored.score,
        coreScore: scored.coreScore,
          keywordScore: scored.keywordScore, semanticScore: scored.semanticScore, ...localRecallExtras(scored),
        characterScore: scored.characterScore,
        rank: relatedIndex + 1,
        candidateCount: relatedCandidates.length,
        matchedTerms:[...(scored.matchedPhrases || []), ...(scored.matchedCoreTokens || []), ...(scored.matchedCharacterTerms || [])],
        matchedCoreTerms:[...(scored.matchedPhrases || []), ...(scored.matchedCoreTokens || []), ...(scored.matchedRareTokens || [])],
        matchedCharacterTerms:[...(scored.matchedCharacterTerms || [])],
      });
      const previous = previousBySource.get(String(b.key));
      const previousTotal = Number(previous?.totalTurns || 0);
      const previousUsed = Number(previous?.usedTurns || 0);
      const previousActive = !!previous && (previousTotal === 0 || previousUsed < previousTotal);
      if (previousActive) next.usedTurns = previousUsed;

      const activeNow = activePendingItems(room.pending);
      const canFit = buildContextBlockFromItems([...activeNow, next]).length <= contextBudgetForCarrier(room, String(room.pending.originalText || '').length);
      if (!canFit) continue;
      nextItems.push(next);
      if (!previousActive) added++;
    }
    return added;
  }

  async function removePendingRecallItem(room, identity, sourceKey = '', autoType = '') {
    const pending = room?.pending;
    const current = pending ? activePendingItems(pending).find(item => pendingItemIdentity(item) === identity) : null;
    const resolvedType = String(current?.autoType || autoType || '');
    const resolvedKey = String(current?.sourceKey || sourceKey || '').replace(/^auto-log:/, '');

    // 직접 선택은 임시 carrier 속성이 아니라 다음 주입에도 유지되는 사용자 설정입니다.
    // 따라서 `빼기`를 누르면 pending 유무와 관계없이 선택 원본에서도 제거합니다.
    if (resolvedType === 'manual-log' && resolvedKey) {
      room.manualLogSelectedKeys = (room.manualLogSelectedKeys || []).map(String).filter(key => key !== resolvedKey);
    }

    if (!pending) {
      if (!resolvedKey) throw new Error('빼 로그를 찾지 못했습니다.');
      if (resolvedType === 'related-log' || resolvedType === 'recent-log' || resolvedType === 'pinned-log') {
        const excluded = new Set((room.autoLogExcludedKeys || []).map(String));
        excluded.add(resolvedKey);
        room.autoLogExcludedKeys = [...excluded];
        if (resolvedType === 'pinned-log') room.autoLogPinnedKeys = (room.autoLogPinnedKeys || []).map(String).filter(key => key !== resolvedKey);
      }
      await saveRoom(room);
      return;
    }

    if (!current) throw new Error('이미 빠졌거나 찾을 수 없는 항목입니다.');
    const removed = quickRemovedPendingItems(pending);
    if (!removed.some(item => pendingItemIdentity(item) === identity)) removed.push({ ...current });
    pending.quickRemovedItems = removed;
    pending.items = (pending.items || []).filter(item => pendingItemIdentity(item) !== identity);
    await syncPendingCarrier(room, 'main-log-remove');
    await saveRoom(room);
  }

  async function applyManualRelatedLog(room, sourceKey, replaceIdentity = '') {
    const slot = (room?.slots || []).find(item => item.id === 'logSummary');
    const blocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(slot?.content || '');
    const block = blocks.find(item => String(item.key) === String(sourceKey));
    if (!slot || !block) throw new Error('선택한 날짜로그를 찾지 못했습니다.');

    const manual = new Set((room.manualLogSelectedKeys || []).map(String));
    const excluded = new Set((room.autoLogExcludedKeys || []).map(String));
    const pending = room.pending;
    const visibleItems = pending ? activePendingItems(pending) : snapshotSelectedItems(room);
    const previous = replaceIdentity
      ? visibleItems.find(item => pendingItemIdentity(item) === replaceIdentity)
      : null;
    const previousKey = String(previous?.sourceKey || '').replace(/^auto-log:/, '');
    if (previous?.autoType === 'manual-log' && previousKey) manual.delete(previousKey);
    if (['related-log','recent-log','pinned-log'].includes(String(previous?.autoType || '')) && previousKey) {
      excluded.add(previousKey);
      if (previous?.autoType === 'pinned-log') {
        room.autoLogPinnedKeys = (room.autoLogPinnedKeys || []).map(String).filter(key => key !== previousKey);
      }
    }
    manual.add(String(block.key));
    excluded.delete(String(block.key));

    if (pending) {
      const next = makeLogRecallItem(block, slot, 'manual-log', '직접주입', '사용자 직접 주입');
      const remaining = activePendingItems(pending).filter(item => {
        const id = pendingItemIdentity(item);
        return id !== replaceIdentity && id !== pendingItemIdentity(next);
      });
      const budget = contextBudgetForCarrier(room, String(pending.originalText || '').length);
      if (buildContextBlockFromItems([...remaining, next]).length > budget) {
        throw new Error('주입 한도를 넘어 이 로그를 추가할 수 없습니다. 다른 항목을 먼저 빼 주세요.');
      }
      if (previous && replaceIdentity) {
        const removed = quickRemovedPendingItems(pending);
        if (!removed.some(item => pendingItemIdentity(item) === replaceIdentity)) removed.push({ ...previous });
        pending.quickRemovedItems = removed;
      }
      pending.quickRemovedItems = quickRemovedPendingItems(pending).filter(item => {
        const key = String(item?.sourceKey || '').replace(/^auto-log:/, '');
        return key !== String(block.key);
      });
      pending.items = [...remaining, next];
      await syncPendingCarrier(room, replaceIdentity ? 'main-related-log-manual-replace' : 'main-related-log-manual-add');
    }

    room.manualLogSelectedKeys = [...manual];
    room.autoLogExcludedKeys = [...excluded];
    await saveRoom(room);
  }

  function openRelatedLogPickerDialog(room, { replaceIdentity = '' } = {}) {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      document.querySelector('#rpcm-related-picker-backdrop')?.remove();
      const slot = (room?.slots || []).find(item => item.id === 'logSummary');
      const blocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(slot?.content || '');
      if (!blocks.length) { notify('선택할 날짜로그가 없습니다.', 'warn'); resolve(null); return; }

      const activeItems = room.pending ? activePendingItems(room.pending) : snapshotSelectedItems(room);
      const activeKeys = new Set(activeItems.filter(item => item.sourceSlotId === 'logSummary' || item.group === 'log-auto').map(item => String(item.sourceKey || '').replace(/^auto-log:/, '')));
      const replacing = activeItems.find(item => pendingItemIdentity(item) === replaceIdentity);
      const replacingKey = String(replacing?.sourceKey || '').replace(/^auto-log:/, '');
      const favorites = new Set((room.favoriteLogKeys || []).map(String));
      const recentKeys = new Set(blocks.slice(-10).map(block => String(block.key)));
      const scored = scoreRelatedLogBlocks(blocks, room.autoRecallContextText || '', new Set(), room);
      const scoreByKey = new Map(scored.map((item, index) => [String(item.block.key), { ...item, rank:index + 1 }]));
      const timelines = [...new Set(blocks.map(logTimelineLabelOfBlock).filter(Boolean))];
      const expanded = new Set();
      const filters = { search:'', date:'', keyword:'', timeline:'', favorites:false, recent:false };
      const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
      backdrop.id = 'rpcm-related-picker-backdrop';
      backdrop.innerHTML = `<div class="rpcm-related-picker" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="관련로그 선택"><header><div><h2>${replaceIdentity ? '관련로그 교체' : '관련로그 추가'}</h2><p>본문을 펼쳐 확인한 뒤 ‘선택’을 누르세요. AI 후보가 아닌 전체 날짜로그도 고를 수 있습니다.</p></div><button type="button" data-related-act="close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">✕</button></header><div class="rpcm-related-picker-filters"><label class="wide"><span>통합 검색</span><input type="search" data-related-filter="search" placeholder="제목·날짜·키워드·본문"></label><label><span>날짜</span><input type="search" data-related-filter="date" placeholder="2027.10.25"></label><label><span>키워드</span><input type="search" data-related-filter="keyword" placeholder="인물·사건"></label><label><span>시간선</span><select data-related-filter="timeline"><option value="">전체 시간선</option>${timelines.map(label => `<option value="${esc(label)}">${esc(label)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label class="check"><input type="checkbox" data-related-filter="favorites"> ★ 즐겨찾기만</label><label class="check"><input type="checkbox" data-related-filter="recent"> 최근 10개만</label></div><div class="rpcm-related-picker-count"></div><div class="rpcm-related-picker-list"></div><footer><span>펼쳐보기는 선택으로 처리되지 않습니다.</span><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-related-act="close">취소</button></footer></div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
      const list = backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-related-picker-list');
      const count = backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-related-picker-count');

      const filteredBlocks = () => blocks.filter(block => {
        const key = String(block.key);
        const heading = String(block.heading || block.titleText || '');
        const content = String(block.raw || '');
        const timeline = logTimelineLabelOfBlock(block);
        const search = filters.search.toLowerCase();
        const date = filters.date.toLowerCase();
        const keyword = filters.keyword.toLowerCase();
        if (search && !`${heading}\n${content}\n${timeline}`.toLowerCase().includes(search)) return false;
        if (date && !`${block.fullDate || ''}\n${heading}`.toLowerCase().includes(date)) return false;
        if (keyword && !`${heading}\n${block.events || ''}\n${content}`.toLowerCase().includes(keyword)) return false;
        if (filters.timeline && normalizedLogTimelineKey(timeline) !== normalizedLogTimelineKey(filters.timeline)) return false;
        if (filters.favorites && !favorites.has(key)) return false;
        if (filters.recent && !recentKeys.has(key)) return false;
        return true;
      }).sort((a, b) => {
        const ar = scoreByKey.get(String(a.key))?.rank || 999999;
        const br = scoreByKey.get(String(b.key))?.rank || 999999;
        return ar - br || Number(b.index || 0) - Number(a.index || 0);
      });

      const renderList = () => {
        const visible = filteredBlocks();
        count.textContent = `${visible.length}/${blocks.length}개 로그${scoreByKey.size ? ' · AI 후보 우선 표시' : ''}`;
        list.innerHTML = visible.length ? visible.map(block => {
          const key = String(block.key);
          const ai = scoreByKey.get(key);
          const already = activeKeys.has(key) && key !== replacingKey;
          const isOpen = expanded.has(key);
          return `<article class="rpcm-related-picker-card${favorites.has(key) ? ' is-favorite' : ''}${already ? ' is-active' : ''}" data-related-key="${esc(key)}"><div class="rpcm-related-picker-row"><button type="button" class="rpcm-related-star" data-related-act="favorite" aria-label="${favorites.has(key) ? '즐겨찾기 해제' : '즐겨찾기 추가'}">${favorites.has(key) ? '★' : '☆'}</button><div><strong>${esc(block.titleText || block.heading || '날짜로그')}</strong><span>${esc(logTimelineLabelOfBlock(block))}${ai ? ` · AI 후보 ${ai.rank}위 · 관련도 ${Number(ai.score || 0).toFixed(1)}` : ''}${already ? ' · 이미 주입 중' : ''}</span></div><button type="button" data-related-act="toggle">${isOpen ? '내용 접기 ▲' : '내용 보기 ▼'}</button><button type="button" class="primary" data-related-act="select" ${already ? 'disabled' : ''}>선택</button></div><pre ${isOpen ? '' : 'hidden'}>${esc(String(block.raw || '').trim())}</pre></article>`;
        }).join('') : '<div class="rpcm-story-empty compact">조건에 맞는 날짜로그가 없습니다.</div>';
      };

      const finish = value => { backdrop.remove(); resolve(value); };
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-related-filter]').forEach(control => {
        const update = () => {
          const name = String(control.dataset.relatedFilter || '');
          filters[name] = control.type === 'checkbox' ? control.checked : String(control.value || '').trim();
          renderList();
        };
        control.addEventListener(control.tagName === 'SELECT' || control.type === 'checkbox' ? 'change' : 'input', update);
      });
      list.addEventListener('click', async event => {
        const button = event.target.closest('button[data-related-act]');
        const card = button?.closest('[data-related-key]');
        if (!button || !card) return;
        const key = String(card.dataset.relatedKey || '');
        const action = button.dataset.relatedAct;
        if (action === 'toggle') { if (expanded.has(key)) expanded.delete(key); else expanded.add(key); renderList(); return; }
        if (action === 'favorite') {
          if (favorites.has(key)) favorites.delete(key); else favorites.add(key);
          room.favoriteLogKeys = [...favorites];
          await saveRoom(room);
          renderList();
          return;
        }
        if (action === 'select') finish(key);
      });
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-related-act="close"]').forEach(button => button.onclick = () => finish(null));
      backdrop.onclick = event => { if (event.target === backdrop) finish(null); };
      backdrop.onkeydown = event => { if (event.key === 'Escape') finish(null); };
      renderList();
    });
  }

  async function replacePendingRelatedLog(room, identity) {
    if(room){prepareLocalRecall(room,room.autoRecallContextText||'');await prepareSemanticRecall(room,room.autoRecallContextText||'');}
    const pending = room?.pending;
    if (!pending) throw new Error('현재 주입 중인 항목이 없습니다.');
    const current = activePendingItems(pending).find(item => pendingItemIdentity(item) === identity && item.autoType === 'related-log');
    if (!current) throw new Error('교체할 관련로그를 찾지 못했습니다.');
    const slot = (room.slots || []).find(item => item.id === 'logSummary');
    const blocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(slot?.content || '');
    const activeKeys = new Set(activePendingItems(pending).filter(item => item.sourceSlotId === 'logSummary' || item.group === 'log-auto').map(item => String(item.sourceKey || '').replace(/^auto-log:/, '')));
    const removedKeys = new Set(quickRemovedPendingItems(pending).map(item => String(item.sourceKey || '').replace(/^auto-log:/, '')));
    const excluded = new Set([...(room.autoLogExcludedKeys || []).map(String), ...activeKeys, ...removedKeys]);
    let candidates = eligibleRelatedLogScores(room, autoRecallVisibleLogBlocks(blocks, room), room.autoRecallContextText || '', excluded);
    if (room.aiContextLogRerankEnabled && candidates.length) {
      const keywordCandidates = candidates;
      try { candidates = await aiRerankRelatedLogCandidates(room, room.autoRecallContextText || '', candidates, 1); }
      catch (error) { recordAiContextFallback(room, error, keywordCandidates, keywordCandidates.slice(0, 1)); candidates = keywordCandidates; }
    }
    const scored = candidates[0];
    if (!scored) throw new Error('현재 맥락에 맞는 다른 관련로그 후보가 없습니다.');
    const rerollMethod = room.aiContextLogRerankEnabled
      ? (room.aiContextReviewReport?.state === 'success' ? 'AI 맥락 검토로 선정' : (semanticEnabled(room)?'AI 검토 실패 후 하이브리드 선정':'AI 검토 실패 후 키워드 선정'))
      : (semanticEnabled(room)?'AI 맥락 검토 OFF · 하이브리드 검색':'AI 맥락 검토 OFF · 기본 검색');
    const next = makeLogRecallItem(scored.block, slot, 'related-log', '관련로그', `${logTimelineLabelOfBlock(scored.block)} · ${rerollMethod} · ${relatedLogReason(scored)}`, {
      score:scored.score, coreScore:scored.coreScore, keywordScore:scored.keywordScore, semanticScore:scored.semanticScore, ...localRecallExtras(scored), characterScore:scored.characterScore, rank:1, candidateCount:candidates.length,
      matchedTerms:[...(scored.matchedPhrases || []), ...(scored.matchedCoreTokens || []), ...(scored.matchedCharacterTerms || [])],
      matchedCoreTerms:[...(scored.matchedPhrases || []), ...(scored.matchedCoreTokens || []), ...(scored.matchedRareTokens || [])],
      matchedCharacterTerms:[...(scored.matchedCharacterTerms || [])],
    });
    const removed = quickRemovedPendingItems(pending);
    if (!removed.some(item => pendingItemIdentity(item) === identity)) removed.push({ ...current });
    pending.quickRemovedItems = removed;
    const index = (pending.items || []).findIndex(item => pendingItemIdentity(item) === identity);
    if (index < 0) throw new Error('교체할 관련로그 위치를 찾지 못했습니다.');
    pending.items[index] = next;
    await syncPendingCarrier(room, 'main-related-log-reroll');
  }

  function autoDetectPersonProfiles(room, cleanFreshText) {
    const found = knowledgeInjectionItems(room, cleanFreshText);
    if (!room.pending) return found.map(item => item.personName);
    const items = Array.isArray(room.pending.items) ? room.pending.items : (room.pending.items = []);
    const removedKeys = new Set(quickRemovedPendingItems(room.pending).map(item => item.slotId));
    const foundIds = new Set(found.map(item => item.slotId));
    room.pending.items = items.filter(item => !isKnowledgePendingItem(item) || foundIds.has(item.slotId));
    for (const item of found) {
      // 빠른 주입 관리에서 제외한 인물은 이 주입 세션 동안 재선택하지 않습니다.
      if (removedKeys.has(item.slotId)) continue;
      const index = room.pending.items.findIndex(existing => existing.slotId === item.slotId);
      if (index < 0) room.pending.items.push(item);
      else room.pending.items[index] = item; // 직접 재등장 시 묶음 전체의 유지 턴을 갱신합니다.
    }
    return found.map(item => item.personName);
  }

  function refreshPendingLoreItems(room, contextText = '') {
    if (!room?.pending) return 0;
    const pending = room.pending;
    const active = activePendingItems(pending);
    const core = active.filter(item => item.group !== 'lore' && item.group !== 'log-auto' && item.sourceSlotId !== 'logSummary');
    const selected = fitLoreItemsToBudget(room, core, loreInjectionCandidates(room, contextText || room.autoRecallContextText || ''), contextBudgetForPreview(room));
    const removed = new Set(quickRemovedPendingItems(pending).map(pendingItemIdentity));
    pending.items = (pending.items || []).filter(item => item.group !== 'lore');
    pending.items.push(...selected.filter(item => !removed.has(pendingItemIdentity(item))));
    return selected.length;
  }

  async function refreshAutomaticMemories(room, recentMessages, freshMessages = null) {
    const fresh = freshMessages || newMessagesSinceLastScan(room, recentMessages);
    if(fresh.length){invalidateAiMemoryHistory();scheduleStoryReviewCountRefresh(room);}
    if (!fresh.length) return { detected: [], added: 0, reset: 0, itemDetected: [], itemReset: 0, logAdded: 0, freshCount: 0 };
    // The newest three user/AI exchanges define the active scene. Keep fresh[] for
    // polling progress, but do not let a one-time historical mention stick forever.
    const sceneMessages = (recentMessages || []).filter(m => ['user','assistant'].includes(messageRoleOf(m))).slice(0, 6);
    const cleanFreshText = sceneMessages.map(m => stripAutomationNoise(messageTextOf(m), true)).filter(Boolean).join('\n');
    room.autoRecallContextText = cleanFreshText.slice(-12000);
    prepareLocalRecall(room,cleanFreshText,sceneMessages);
    await prepareSemanticRecall(room,cleanFreshText,sceneMessages);
    const charResult = await autoDetectCharacters(room, sceneMessages);
    const itemResult = autoDetectImportantItems(room, sceneMessages);
    const personDetected = autoDetectPersonProfiles(room, cleanFreshText);
    refreshPendingLoreItems(room, cleanFreshText);
    refreshAutoRecentLogsToPending(room);
    const logAdded = await addAutoRelatedLogsToPending(room, cleanFreshText);
    await saveRoom(room);
    if (room.chatId === state.currentChatId) state.currentRoom = room;
    return { ...charResult, itemDetected:itemResult.detected, itemReset:itemResult.reset, personDetected, logAdded, freshCount: fresh.length };
  }

  function injectionRecheckSnapshot(room){
    return structuredClone(room.pending?activePendingItems(room.pending):snapshotSelectedItems(room));
  }
  function injectionRecheckMethod(item){
    if(item.group==='lore')return item.loreMode==='auto'?(Number(item.loreSemanticScore)>=SEMANTIC_SEARCH.threshold?'로컬 + Gemini 의미검색':'로컬 검색'):'사용자 주입 설정';
    if(item.autoType==='related-log'){const search=Number(item.recallSemanticScore)>=SEMANTIC_SEARCH.threshold?'로컬 + Gemini 의미검색':'로컬 검색';return /AI 맥락 검토로 선정|AI 맥락 검토 ·/.test(item.recallReason||'')?search+' → AI 맥락 검토':search;}
    if(item.group==='item'||item.group==='character'||item.autoType==='person')return '로컬 RP 언급 감지';
    if(['recent-log','pinned-log','manual-log'].includes(item.autoType))return '최근 개수·사용자 선택';
    return '사용자 주입 설정';
  }
  function buildInjectionRecheckReport(room,before,after,context){
    const old=new Map(before.map(row=>[pendingItemIdentity(row),row])),next=new Map(after.map(row=>[pendingItemIdentity(row),row]));
    const suppressed=new Set(quickRemovedPendingItems(room.pending).map(pendingItemIdentity));
    const lore=new Map(loreInjectionCandidates(room,context).map(row=>[pendingItemIdentity(row),row]));
    const logs=parseDatedLogBlocks(room.slots.find(row=>row.id==='logSummary')?.content||'');
    const logCandidates=new Map(eligibleRelatedLogScores(room,autoRecallVisibleLogBlocks(logs,room),context,new Set((room.autoLogExcludedKeys||[]).map(String))).map(entry=>['log:'+entry.block.key,entry]));
    const semantic=semanticRoom(room),vectors=semanticScoresForContext(room,context,'log').size+semanticScoresForContext(room,context,'lore').size;
    const search=vectors?`로컬 + Gemini 의미검색 · 유효 자료 ${vectors}개 / 전체 ${semanticDocuments(room).length}개`:`로컬 검색 · ${semanticStatus(room)}`;
    const changes=[];
    for(const [id,item]of old){
      if(next.has(id))continue;
      let reason='',method='주입 설정 확인';
      if(suppressed.has(id))reason='사용자가 이번 주입에서 제외한 항목';
      else if(item.totalTurns>0&&item.usedTurns>=item.totalTurns)reason='유지 턴이 끝남';
      else if(item.group==='item'||item.group==='character'){
        method='로컬 RP 언급 감지';const slot=room.slots.find(row=>row.id===item.slotId);
        reason=slot?.manualInjectionOverride==='off'||slot?.autoExcluded?'사용자 자동 주입 제외 설정':slot?.enabled===false?'최근 RP에서 감지 조건을 충족하지 못해 자동 선택 해제':'현재 주입 용량에 맞추는 과정에서 제외';
      }else if(item.autoType==='person') {method='로컬 RP 언급 감지';reason='최근 RP의 인물 언급·주입 조건을 충족하지 못함';}
      else if(item.group==='lore'){
        const candidate=lore.get(id);method=candidate?injectionRecheckMethod(candidate):(vectors?'로컬 + Gemini 의미검색':'로컬 검색');
        reason=candidate?'검색 후보에는 포함됐으나 자료집 개수·주입 용량 제한에서 제외':'팩·카드 설정 또는 현재 문맥의 검색 기준을 충족하지 못함';
      }else if(item.sourceSlotId==='logSummary'||item.group==='log-auto'){
        const key=String(item.sourceKey||'');
        if((room.autoLogExcludedKeys||[]).includes(key)){reason='사용자가 자동 주입에서 제외한 날짜로그';}
        else if(item.autoType==='recent-log'){method='최근 날짜로그 개수';reason='설정된 최근 날짜로그 범위 밖으로 이동';}
        else{const hit=logCandidates.get(id);method=hit&&hit.semanticScore>=SEMANTIC_SEARCH.threshold?'로컬 + Gemini 의미검색':vectors?'로컬 + Gemini 의미검색':'로컬 검색';const decision=room.aiContextReviewReport?.state==='success'?room.aiContextReviewReport.candidates?.find(row=>row.key===key):null;if(decision)method+=' → AI 맥락 검토';reason=decision&&!decision.selected?'AI 맥락 검토에서 미선택: '+decision.reason:decision?.selected?'AI 선정 후 관련로그 개수·주입 용량 제한에서 제외':hit?'현재 후보 순위·관련로그 개수 또는 주입 용량 제한에서 제외':'현재 문맥의 관련로그 검색 조건을 충족하지 못함';}
      }else reason='현재 항목의 주입 선택·유지 설정에 따라 제외';
      changes.push({title:item.title||item.slotId,change:'제외',method,reason,previousReason:String(item.recallReason||'')});
    }
    for(const [id,item]of next){const previous=old.get(id);if(!previous||previous.content!==item.content)changes.push({title:item.title||item.slotId,change:previous?'내용 갱신':'추가',method:injectionRecheckMethod(item),reason:String(item.recallReason||item.autoDetectionReason||'현재 주입 설정에 따라 선택')});}
    const ai=room.aiContextReviewReport;
    return {at:nowIso(),pending:!!room.pending,search,aiReview:room.aiContextLogRerankEnabled?ai?.state==='success'?'AI 맥락 검토 성공':ai?.state==='fallback'?'AI 맥락 검토 실패 · 검색 결과 사용':ai?.state==='skipped'?'AI 맥락 검토 미실행 · '+ai.note:'AI 맥락 검토 설정 ON · 실행 결과는 관련로그 상세에서 확인':'AI 맥락 검토 OFF',before:before.length,after:after.length,changes};
  }
  function recordRecallSelectionAudit(room,before,after,context,trigger,verified){
    const report=buildInjectionRecheckReport(room,before,after,context);
    report.trigger=trigger;report.serverVerified=verified;report.messageId=verified?String(room.pending?.messageId||''):'';
    report.logs=after.filter(row=>row.sourceSlotId==='logSummary'||row.group==='log-auto').map(row=>({title:row.title,method:injectionRecheckMethod(row),reason:row.recallReason||'사용자 설정'}));
    room.recallSelectionHistory=[...(room.recallSelectionHistory||[]),report].slice(-20);room.injectionRecheckReport=report;
    return report;
  }
  function wishStatusBadge(label,tone='neutral') {
    return `<span class="rpcm-status-pill tone-${esc(tone)}">${esc(label)}</span>`;
  }
  function recallTriggerLabel(trigger) {
    const text=String(trigger||'');
    const names={'manual-ai-context-refresh':'AI 맥락 수동 검토','manual-latest-rp-refresh':'최신 RP 주입 재검사','quick-panel':'주입 항목 변경','manual-add':'주입 항목 추가','manual-remove':'주입 항목 제외','main-log-remove':'날짜로그 제외','main-related-log-manual-replace':'관련로그 교체','main-related-log-manual-add':'관련로그 직접 추가','main-related-log-reroll':'관련로그 다시 선택','full-rebuild':'전체 재구축 반영','integrated-continue':'통합 이어갱신 반영','retention-change':'유지 턴 변경','delete-slot':'기억 삭제','delete-selected-items':'물품 삭제','person-bundles-edit':'인물정보 변경','log-recall-upgrade':'날짜로그 설정 반영'};
    if(names[text])return names[text];
    if(/^manual-update-/.test(text))return '수동 업데이트 반영';
    if(/^ai-memory-/.test(text))return 'AI 기억정리 반영';
    return /[가-힣]/.test(text)?text:'주입 구성 갱신';
  }
  function renderRecallExecutionHistory(room){
    const history=(room.recallSelectionHistory||[]).slice(-10).reverse(),refiners=(room.responseRefinerHistory||[]).slice(-10).reverse();
    const rows=history.map(row=>{
      const changes=row.changes||[],removed=changes.filter(c=>c.change==='제외').length,added=changes.length-removed;
      return `<details class="rpcm-audit-entry"><summary><span class="rpcm-audit-title">${esc(recallTriggerLabel(row.trigger))}<time>${esc(new Date(row.at).toLocaleString('ko-KR'))}</time></span><span class="rpcm-badge-row">${wishStatusBadge(row.serverVerified?'서버 주입 확인':'미리보기',row.serverVerified?'success':'neutral')}${wishStatusBadge('추가·갱신 '+added,added?'accent':'neutral')}${wishStatusBadge('제외 '+removed,removed?'warning':'neutral')}</span></summary><div class="rpcm-audit-entry-body"><div class="rpcm-audit-route"><span><b>검색</b>${esc(row.search)}</span><span><b>AI 검토</b>${esc(row.aiReview)}</span></div>${changes.map(c=>`<article class="rpcm-audit-change"><header>${wishStatusBadge(c.change,c.change==='제외'?'warning':'accent')}<strong>${esc(c.title)}</strong></header><p>${esc(c.reason)}</p><small>${esc(c.method)}</small></article>`).join('')||'<p class="rpcm-audit-empty">선택 변경 없음 · 현재 구성을 유지했습니다.</p>'}<details class="rpcm-audit-kept"><summary>유지된 날짜로그 ${wishStatusBadge((row.logs||[]).length+'개')}</summary>${(row.logs||[]).map(c=>`<article class="rpcm-audit-change"><strong>${esc(c.title)}</strong><p>${esc(c.reason)}</p><small>${esc(c.method)}</small></article>`).join('')||'<p class="rpcm-audit-empty">주입 날짜로그 없음</p>'}</details></div></details>`;
    }).join('');
    const ref=refiners.map(row=>{
      const type=String(row.type||''),label=type==='PASS'?'이상 없음':/ERROR|오류|실패/.test(type)?'검수 실패':/APPLIED|적용|교정 완료/.test(type)?'교정 완료':/STALE|변경|만료/.test(type)?'재검수 필요':/[가-힣]/.test(type)?type:'검수 결과';
      return `<details class="rpcm-audit-entry"><summary><span class="rpcm-audit-title">응답교정<time>${esc(new Date(row.at).toLocaleString('ko-KR'))}</time></span><span class="rpcm-badge-row">${wishStatusBadge(label,type==='PASS'?'success':/ERROR|오류|실패/.test(type)?'warning':'neutral')}${wishStatusBadge(((Number(row.timing?.requestMs)||0)/1000).toFixed(1)+'초')}</span></summary><div class="rpcm-audit-entry-body"><p>${esc(row.details||row.verdict||'검수 완료 · 변경 없음')}</p><div class="rpcm-audit-route"><span><b>연결</b>${esc(row.provider||'')} · ${esc(row.model||'')}</span><span><b>입력</b>${Number(row.timing?.promptChars)||0}자</span></div><details class="rpcm-audit-kept"><summary>실행 정보</summary><small>상태 ${esc(type)} · 응답 ${esc(row.messageId||'')}</small></details></div></details>`;
    }).join('');
    return `<article class="rpcm-pref-card rpcm-ai-execution-history"><header class="rpcm-audit-heading"><div><h3>검색·주입 실행 기록</h3><p>무엇을 넣고 뺐는지, 실제 서버에 반영됐는지 확인하세요.</p></div>${wishStatusBadge(history.length+'개 기록')}</header><p class="rpcm-audit-search-status">${esc(semanticStatus(room))}</p><div class="rpcm-audit-list">${rows||'<p class="rpcm-audit-empty">아직 기록이 없습니다. 주입 재검사 또는 AI 다시 검토를 실행하면 기록됩니다.</p>'}</div><details class="rpcm-audit-refiners"><summary><strong>AI 응답교정</strong>${wishStatusBadge('최근 '+refiners.length+'개')}</summary><p class="rpcm-audit-hint">응답교정은 AI 답변을 검수하며 날짜로그 선택을 직접 바꾸지 않습니다.</p><div class="rpcm-audit-list">${ref||'<p class="rpcm-audit-empty">이 방의 응답교정 실행 기록이 없습니다.</p>'}</div></details></article>`;
  }

  function renderAiContextReviewDetails(room,report,recentCount,relatedShown,totalLogs,runMeta,situation){
    const tone=report?.state==='success'?'success':report?.state==='fallback'?'warning':'neutral';
    const label=report?.state==='success'?'검토 완료':report?.state==='fallback'?'검색으로 대체':report?.state==='skipped'?'미실행':'검토 전';
    const counts=[['전체 로그',report?.totalLogs??totalLogs],['검색 후보',report?.candidateCount||0],['AI 검토',report?.inspectedCount||0],['AI 선정',report?.selectedCount??relatedShown]];
    const selected=(report?.items||[]).map(item=>`<article class="rpcm-audit-change"><header>${wishStatusBadge(item.rank+'위','accent')}<strong>${esc(item.title)}</strong></header><p>${esc(report.candidates?.find(row=>row.key===item.key)?.reason||item.reason||'')}</p></article>`).join('');
    return `<details class="rpcm-ai-context-report"><summary><strong>AI 검토 상세</strong><span class="rpcm-badge-row">${wishStatusBadge(label,tone)}${wishStatusBadge('관련 '+relatedShown+'개')}</span></summary><div class="rpcm-context-review-body"><div class="rpcm-ai-context-actions"><time>${esc(report?.checkedAt?new Date(report.checkedAt).toLocaleString('ko-KR'):'아직 실행하지 않았습니다.')}</time><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small rpcm-ai-context-refresh" id="rpcm-ai-context-refresh">↻ AI 다시 검토</button></div><div class="rpcm-context-metrics">${counts.map(([name,value])=>`<div><span>${esc(name)}</span><strong>${Number(value)}<small>개</small></strong></div>`).join('')}</div><div class="rpcm-ai-context-situation"><strong>현재 RP 상황</strong><span>${esc(situation)}</span></div>${report?.note?`<p class="rpcm-context-note">${esc(report.note)}</p>`:''}${report?.failureCategory?`<p class="rpcm-context-note is-warning">${esc(report.failureCategory)} · ${esc(report.failureDetail||'')}</p>`:''}${selected?`<div class="rpcm-audit-list">${selected}</div>`:!report?.note?'<p class="rpcm-audit-empty">선택한 과거 후보가 없습니다. 최근 연속성 로그는 별도로 유지합니다.</p>':''}${report?.candidates?.length?`<details class="rpcm-audit-kept"><summary>후보별 선택 이유 ${wishStatusBadge(report.candidates.length+'개')}</summary>${report.candidates.map(row=>`<article class="rpcm-audit-change"><header>${wishStatusBadge(row.selected?'선택':'미선택',row.selected?'accent':'neutral')}<strong>${esc(row.title)}</strong></header><p>${esc(row.reason)}</p><small>키워드 ${Number(row.keywordScore||0).toFixed(1)} · 의미 유사도 ${Math.round(Number(row.semanticScore||0)*100)}%</small></article>`).join('')}</details>`:''}<details class="rpcm-audit-kept"><summary>연결·실행 정보</summary><p>${esc(runMeta)}<br>${esc(report?.provider||'')} · ${esc(report?.model||'')}</p>${report?.parseFailure?`<p>${esc(report.parseFailure)}</p>`:''}${report?.failureRaw?`<pre>${esc(report.failureRaw)}</pre>`:''}</details></div></details>`;
  }

  function renderInjectionRecheckReport(room){
    const r=room?.injectionRecheckReport;if(!r)return '<p class="rpcm-recheck-empty">재검사 후 제외·추가한 항목과 이유가 여기에 남습니다.</p>';
    return `<details class="rpcm-recheck-report" data-injection-recheck-report><summary>최근 재검사 결과 · 제외 ${r.changes.filter(row=>row.change==='제외').length} · 추가/갱신 ${r.changes.filter(row=>row.change!=='제외').length}<small>${esc(new Date(r.at).toLocaleString('ko-KR'))}</small></summary><p>${esc(r.search)}<br>${esc(r.aiReview)}<br>${r.pending?'현재 주입':'다음 주입 후보'} ${r.before} → ${r.after}개</p>${r.changes.length?r.changes.map(row=>`<article><header><strong>${esc(row.title)}</strong><span>${esc(row.change)}</span></header><small>${esc(row.method)}</small><p>${esc(row.reason)}</p>${row.previousReason?`<details><summary>이전 선택 이유</summary><p>${esc(row.previousReason)}</p></details>`:''}</article>`).join(''):'<p>추가하거나 제외한 항목이 없습니다.</p>'}<small>인물·물품 감지는 로컬 판정입니다. Gemini 의미검색은 날짜로그·자동 자료집 후보에만 사용합니다. 개수·용량 제한은 검색 방식과 별도로 적용됩니다.</small></details>`;
  }

  async function refreshInjectionSelectionFromLatestRp(room) {
    if (!room || state.currentRoom !== room) throw new Error('현재 RP 방이 바뀌었습니다. 다시 열고 시도해 주세요.');
    const before = injectionRecheckSnapshot(room);
    const recent = await fetchRecentMessages(apiChatIdOf(room), Math.max(Number(APP.autoScanMessageLimit || 0), 12));
    const sceneMessages = (recent || []).filter(message => ['user','assistant'].includes(messageRoleOf(message))).slice(0, 6);
    if (!sceneMessages.length) throw new Error('최신 RP 메시지를 찾지 못했습니다.');

    const cleanText = sceneMessages
      .map(message => stripAutomationNoise(messageTextOf(message), true))
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join('\n\n');
    if (!cleanText.trim()) throw new Error('최신 RP 본문을 읽지 못했습니다.');

    room.autoRecallContextText = cleanText.slice(-12000);
    prepareLocalRecall(room,cleanText,sceneMessages);
    await prepareSemanticRecall(room,cleanText,sceneMessages);
    const newest = sceneMessages[0];
    if (newest) room.autoScanLastMessageId = String(messageIdOf(newest) || room.autoScanLastMessageId || '');

    const charResult = await autoDetectCharacters(room, sceneMessages);
    const itemResult = autoDetectImportantItems(room, sceneMessages);
    const personDetected = autoDetectPersonProfiles(room, cleanText);

    let loreCount = 0, logAdded = 0;
    let slotSync = { added:0, removed:0, updated:0 };
    if (room.pending) {
      // 체크박스/자동 판정과 기존 carrier를 먼저 완전히 대조합니다.
      // 특히 이전 버전에서 남은 기타·OOC/캐릭터/물품 항목이 현재 OFF인데도 carrier에 남는 문제를 제거합니다.
      slotSync = reconcilePendingSlotsWithCurrentSelection(room);
      loreCount = refreshPendingLoreItems(room, cleanText);
      refreshAutoRecentLogsToPending(room);
      logAdded = await addAutoRelatedLogsToPending(room, cleanText);
      // 현재 주입에서 사용자가 빠르게 제외한 항목은 새로고침이 임의로 되살리지 않습니다.
      applyQuickItemSuppression(room.pending);
      await saveRoom(room);
      await syncPendingCarrier(room, 'manual-latest-rp-refresh');
    } else {
      // Review the next carrier selection without claiming any server write.
      const budget=contextBudgetForPreview(room);
      const preview=await rerankInitialRelatedLogItems(room,snapshotSelectedItems(room,cleanText,budget),cleanText,budget,{manual:true});
      recordRecallSelectionAudit(room,before,preview,cleanText,'최신 RP 주입 재검사 · 다음 주입 후보',false);
      await saveRoom(room);
    }

    if(room.pending)room.injectionRecheckReport=buildInjectionRecheckReport(room,before,injectionRecheckSnapshot(room),cleanText);
    await saveRoom(room);
    if (room.chatId === state.currentChatId) state.currentRoom = room;
    return {
      report:room.injectionRecheckReport,
      characters:charResult.detected?.length || 0,
      items:itemResult.detected?.length || 0,
      people:Array.isArray(personDetected) ? personDetected.length : 0,
      lore:loreCount,
      logs:logAdded,
      pending:!!room.pending,
      slotSync,
    };
  }

  async function armInjectionNow(room) {
    if (room.pending) throw new Error('이미 자동 유지 컨텍스트가 활성화되어 있습니다. 먼저 지금 해제해 주세요.');

    const recentForAuto = await fetchRecentMessages(apiChatIdOf(room), APP.autoScanMessageLimit);
    await refreshAutomaticMemories(room, recentForAuto, recentForAuto.filter(m => ['user','assistant'].includes(messageRoleOf(m))).slice(0, APP.autoScanMessageLimit));
    const firstAutoMessage = recentForAuto.find(m => ['user','assistant'].includes(messageRoleOf(m)));
    if (firstAutoMessage) room.autoScanLastMessageId = String(messageIdOf(firstAutoMessage) || room.autoScanLastMessageId || '');
    const recallText = recentForAuto.filter(m => ['user','assistant'].includes(messageRoleOf(m))).slice(0,6)
      .map(m => stripAutomationNoise(messageTextOf(m),true)).filter(Boolean).join('\n');
    room.autoRecallContextText = recallText.slice(-12000);
    prepareLocalRecall(room,recallText,recentForAuto);
    await prepareSemanticRecall(room,recallText,recentForAuto);
    const recent = await fetchRecentMessages(apiChatIdOf(room), 40);
    if (!recent.length) throw new Error('채팅 메시지를 찾지 못했습니다.');
    const newest = recent[0];
    if (messageRoleOf(newest) !== 'assistant') throw new Error('가장 최근 메시지가 AI 응답이 아닙니다. AI 생성이 끝난 뒤 주입을 시작해 주세요.');
    const latestAssistant = recent.find(m => messageRoleOf(m) === 'assistant');
    if (!latestAssistant) throw new Error('컨텍스트를 붙일 AI 메시지가 없습니다.');

    const targetId = messageIdOf(latestAssistant);
    if (!targetId) throw new Error('AI 메시지 ID를 찾지 못했습니다.');
    const rawOriginal = messageTextOf(latestAssistant);
    if (!rawOriginal) throw new Error('최근 AI 메시지 원문을 읽지 못했습니다.');
    const cleanOriginal = stripOurContextBlock(rawOriginal).text || rawOriginal;
    const initialContextBudget = contextBudgetForCarrier(room, cleanOriginal.length);
    let items = snapshotSelectedItems(room, recallText, initialContextBudget);
    // 최초 주입에는 아직 pending이 없으므로, 갱신 경로와 별도로 AI 맥락 검토를 적용합니다.
    // API가 실패하면 이미 계산된 로컬 키워드 결과를 그대로 사용합니다.
    items = await rerankInitialRelatedLogItems(room, items, recallText, initialContextBudget);
    items = fitItemsToCarrierLimits(room, cleanOriginal, items);
    const contextBlock = buildContextBlockFromItems(items);
    if (!contextBlock) throw new Error('주입할 항목이 없습니다. 현재상태/캐릭터/중요 물품/자료집/기타 또는 날짜 로그의 직접 주입·최근·관련 자동 선택 설정을 확인해 주세요.');
    const injectedText = buildInjectedMessage(cleanOriginal, contextBlock);
    const requestMetrics = assertCarrierRequestWithinLimits(room, injectedText);

    const pending = {
      messageId: targetId,
      originalText: cleanOriginal,
      baselineAssistantId: targetId,
      sessionStartedAt: Date.now(), armedAt: Date.now(), carrierArmedAt: Date.now(),
      injectedChars: contextBlock.length, originalChars: cleanOriginal.length, carrierChars: injectedText.length,
      carrierMessageBytes:requestMetrics.messageBytes, carrierPayloadBytes:requestMetrics.payloadBytes,
      carrierRole: 'assistant', mode: 'append-hidden-html-comment-reanchor-per-item',
      verified: false, verifiedAt: null, serverChars: 0,
      logRecallRevision: APP.logRecallRevision,
      contextBlock, items,
    };
    stampVerifiedPendingSnapshot(pending, items, contextBlock);

    savePendingBackup(room.chatId, pending);
    await patchMessage(apiChatIdOf(room), targetId, injectedText);
    const verification = await verifyInjectedCarrier(room, pending, injectedText);
    if (!verification.verified) {
      try {
        await restoreCarrierOnly(room, pending);
        clearPendingBackup(room.chatId);
      } catch (rollbackError) {
        // 롤백을 확인하지 못한 경우 복구 가능한 pending/백업을 반드시 남깁니다.
        room.pending = pending;
        savePendingBackup(room.chatId, pending);
        await saveRoom(room);
        throw new Error(`서버 주입 검증과 원문 롤백 확인에 실패했습니다. 복구 정보는 유지했습니다: ${rollbackError.message}`);
      }
      throw new Error('서버 재확인에서 숨김 컨텍스트를 확인하지 못해 AI 원문으로 안전 복원했습니다. 주입 해제 후 다시 주입해 주세요.');
    }
    pending.verified = true; pending.verifiedAt = Date.now(); pending.serverChars = verification.serverChars;
    stampVerifiedPendingSnapshot(pending, items, contextBlock);
    savePendingBackup(room.chatId, pending);
    room.pending = pending;recordRecallSelectionAudit(room,[],items,recallText,'주입 시작',true); await saveRoom(room);
    if (room.chatId === state.currentChatId) state.currentRoom = room;
    scheduleRecovery(APP.activePollMs);
    sanitizeRenderedContextSoon();
    notify(`서버 주입 확인됨 ✓ · ${items.length}개 항목 독립 유지 시작`, 'success', 6500);
    renderModalIfOpen();
  }

  function armInjection(room) {
    return queuePendingCarrierOperation(room, () => armInjectionNow(room));
  }

  async function restorePendingNow(room, reason = 'manual') {
    const p = room.pending;
    if (!p) return { restored: false, reason: 'none' };

    // 네트워크/PATCH 오류 때 finally에서 백업을 지우면 서버에는 숨김 블록이 남고
    // 복원 정보만 사라질 수 있습니다. 복원 또는 이미 정리됨이 확인된 뒤에만 해제합니다.
    const result = await restoreCarrierOnly(room, p);
    room.pending = null;
    clearPendingBackup(room.chatId);
    await saveRoom(room);

    if (room.chatId === state.currentChatId) {
      state.currentRoom = room;
      if (reason === 'manual') notifyInjectionEnded(room, 'manual');
      else if (reason === 'completed') notifyInjectionEnded(room, 'completed');
      else notify(`숨김 컨텍스트 원문 복원 완료 · ${reason === 'recovery' ? '복구 처리' : reason}`, 'success', 5000);
      renderModalIfOpen();
    }
    return result;
  }

  function restorePending(room, reason = 'manual') {
    return queuePendingCarrierOperation(room, () => restorePendingNow(room, reason));
  }

  async function reanchorAfterResponseNow(room, latestAssistant, options = {}) {
    const p = room.pending;
    const recallBefore=clonePendingItems(p?.lastVerifiedItems||p?.items||[]);
    if (!p) return;
    const countTurn = options.countTurn !== false;
    const rerollMove = options.reason === 'reroll';
    const newId = messageIdOf(latestAssistant);
    if (!newId || newId === p.messageId) return;
    await sleep(APP.reanchorDelayMs);

    // 이전 carrier 원문 복원
    await restoreCarrierOnly(room, p);

    // 일반 새 응답만 유지 횟수를 차감합니다. 리롤은 같은 USER 턴의 대체 답변이므로
    // 컨텍스트 위치만 옮기고 유지 횟수는 그대로 둡니다.
    const items = Array.isArray(p.items) ? p.items : [];
    if (countTurn) {
      for (const item of items) {
        const total = Number(item.totalTurns || 0);
        if (total !== 0 && Number(item.usedTurns || 0) < total) item.usedTurns = Number(item.usedTurns || 0) + 1;
      }
    }

    // v0.8.9에서 이어진 pending은 최근로그가 저장소 뒤쪽 순서로 고정되어 있을 수 있습니다.
    // 새 carrier로 옮기기 전에 한 번만 현재 로그 저장소 기준으로 다시 선정합니다.
    if (Number(p.logRecallRevision || 0) < APP.logRecallRevision) {
      replacePendingLogItems(room);
      refreshPendingLoreItems(room, room.autoRecallContextText || '');
    }

    // 방금 완료된 USER→AI 흐름에서 새 등장 캐릭터/관련 과거로그를 찾아 다음 응답용 컨텍스트에 추가합니다.
    const recentForAuto = await fetchRecentMessages(apiChatIdOf(room), APP.autoScanMessageLimit);
    const autoResult = countTurn
      ? await refreshAutomaticMemories(room, recentForAuto)
      : { detected: [], added: 0, reset: 0, itemDetected: [], itemReset: 0, logAdded: 0, freshCount: 0 };
    if (room.pending) room.pending.items = p.items;
    // 0.16.1: a legacy pending snapshot may contain auto picks from weeks ago.
    // Do not retain it without a fresh mention merely because a retention counter remains.
    p.items = (p.items || []).filter(item => {
      if (!['item','character'].includes(item.group)) return true;
      const slot = (room.slots || []).find(s => s.id === item.slotId);
      return !slot || rpShouldInjectSlot(room,slot);
    });
    refreshAutoRecentLogsToPending(room);
    const persistenceRepair = ensureDirectReleasePendingItems(room, p);
    if (persistenceRepair.added) console.info('[RP매니저] 직접 해제 항목 유지 복구:', persistenceRepair.added);
    applyQuickItemSuppression(p);
    let active = activePendingItems(p);
    if (!active.length) {
      room.pending = null; clearPendingBackup(room.chatId); await saveRoom(room);
      if (room.chatId === state.currentChatId) {
        state.currentRoom = room;
        notifyInjectionEnded(room, 'completed');
        renderModalIfOpen();
      }
      return;
    }

    const fresh = await fetchMessage(apiChatIdOf(room), newId) || latestAssistant;
    if (messageRoleOf(fresh) !== 'assistant') throw new Error('새 carrier가 AI 메시지가 아니어서 자동 유지를 중단합니다.');
    const newRaw = messageTextOf(fresh);
    if (!newRaw) throw new Error('새 AI 응답 원문을 읽지 못해 자동 유지를 중단합니다.');
    const stripped = stripOurContextBlock(newRaw);
    const newOriginal = stripped.found ? stripped.text : newRaw;
    active = fitItemsToCarrierLimits(room, newOriginal, active);
    if (!active.length) {
      room.pending = null; clearPendingBackup(room.chatId); await saveRoom(room);
      if (room.chatId === state.currentChatId) { state.currentRoom = room; notifyInjectionEnded(room, 'empty'); renderModalIfOpen(); }
      return;
    }
    const activeLogIds = new Set(active.filter(i => i.sourceSlotId === 'logSummary' || i.group === 'log-auto' || i.slotId === 'logSummary').map(i => i.slotId));
    const activeLoreIds = new Set(active.filter(i => i.group === 'lore').map(i => i.slotId));
    const activeLoreById = new Map(active.filter(i => i.group === 'lore').map(i => [i.slotId, i]));
    p.items = (p.items || []).filter(i => i.group === 'lore' ? activeLoreIds.has(i.slotId) : !(i.sourceSlotId === 'logSummary' || i.group === 'log-auto' || i.slotId === 'logSummary') || activeLogIds.has(i.slotId)).map(i => i.group === 'lore' ? (activeLoreById.get(i.slotId) || i) : i);
    const contextBlock = buildContextBlockFromItems(active);
    if (!contextBlock) throw new Error('유지할 활성 컨텍스트가 없어 자동 유지를 중단합니다.');
    const nextInjected = buildInjectedMessage(newOriginal, contextBlock);
    const requestMetrics = carrierRequestMetrics(nextInjected);
    const maxChars = Number(room.maxChars) || APP.defaultMaxChars;
    if (nextInjected.length > maxChars || requestMetrics.payloadBytes > APP.safeCarrierPayloadBytes) {
      throw new Error(`새 AI 응답 + 숨김 컨텍스트가 주입 안전선을 넘어 이번 carrier 이동을 보류했습니다. 설정과 계속 유지 항목은 보존하며 다음 답변에서 다시 시도합니다. ${formatCount(nextInjected.length)} / ${formatCount(maxChars)}자 · UTF-8 요청 ${formatCount(requestMetrics.payloadBytes)} / ${formatCount(APP.safeCarrierPayloadBytes)}바이트`);
    }

    const nextPending = { ...p, messageId: newId, originalText: newOriginal, baselineAssistantId: newId,
      armedAt: Date.now(), carrierArmedAt: Date.now(), originalChars: newOriginal.length, carrierChars: nextInjected.length,
      carrierMessageBytes:requestMetrics.messageBytes, carrierPayloadBytes:requestMetrics.payloadBytes,
      verified: false, verifiedAt: null, serverChars: 0, logRecallRevision: APP.logRecallRevision, contextBlock, injectedChars: contextBlock.length, items: p.items };
    delete nextPending.reanchorRetry;
    stampVerifiedPendingSnapshot(nextPending, active, contextBlock);
    if (rerollMove) delete nextPending.reroll;

    await patchMessage(apiChatIdOf(room), newId, nextInjected);
    const verification = await verifyInjectedCarrier(room, nextPending, nextInjected);
    if (!verification.verified) {
      try {
        await restoreCarrierOnly(room, nextPending);
      } catch (rollbackError) {
        room.pending = nextPending;
        savePendingBackup(room.chatId, nextPending);
        await saveRoom(room);
        throw new Error(`새 carrier 검증과 원문 롤백 확인에 실패했습니다. 복구 정보는 유지했습니다: ${rollbackError.message}`);
      }
      throw new Error('새 carrier 서버 검증에 실패해 새 AI 원문으로 안전 복원했습니다. 설정과 계속 유지 항목은 보존하며 자동으로 다시 시도합니다.');
    }
    nextPending.verified = true; nextPending.verifiedAt = Date.now(); nextPending.serverChars = verification.serverChars;
    stampVerifiedPendingSnapshot(nextPending, active, contextBlock);
    room.pending = nextPending;recordRecallSelectionAudit(room,recallBefore,active,room.autoRecallContextText||'','완료 RP 이후 자동 주입 갱신',true); savePendingBackup(room.chatId, nextPending); await saveRoom(room);
    if (room.chatId === state.currentChatId) {
      state.currentRoom = room;
      sanitizeRenderedContextSoon();
      const autoBits = [];
      if (autoResult?.detected?.length) autoBits.push(`캐릭터 ${autoResult.detected.map(x => x.slot.title).join(', ')}`);
      if (autoResult?.itemDetected?.length) autoBits.push(`중요 물품 ${autoResult.itemDetected.map(x => x.slot.title).join(', ')}`);
      if (autoResult?.logAdded) autoBits.push(`관련로그 ${autoResult.logAdded}개`);
      notify(`${rerollMove ? '리롤 컨텍스트 유지 완료' : '컨텍스트 자동 이동 완료'} ✓ · ${active.length}개 항목 유지 중${autoBits.length ? ` · 자동호출 ${autoBits.join(' / ')}` : ''}`, 'context-move', autoBits.length ? 4800 : 3200);
      renderModalIfOpen();
    }
  }

  function reanchorAfterResponse(room, latestAssistant, options = {}) {
    return queuePendingCarrierOperation(room, () => reanchorAfterResponseNow(room, latestAssistant, options));
  }

  async function checkPendingRoom(room) {
    const p = room.pending;
    if (!p) return;

    const recent = await fetchRecentMessages(apiChatIdOf(room), 20);
